## 2026-11-19 — Focused document selection, command scope and compact projection

Added pane replacement and selection semantics without creating tabs: visible existing tabs receive focus, other existing tabs replace only the focused leaf, empty layouts restore using a supplied pane ID. PaneLayoutHistory.select preserves focus-only redo and records replacements. Minimum sizes now key on TAB ID, so replacing a small document with a larger one validates the candidate's own constraint. This is a pre-merge model API correction; no app caller/storage integration exists to migrate.

PaneCommandTarget captures window/live-workspace/pane/tab identity; accepts defaults to requiring current focus, while explicitly nonfocused checks allow only that still-visible document's own viewport save. Stale/replaced/closed/foreign targets reject. This is not a navigation revision, security permission or generation token; future operation-specific cancellation/revision checks remain required, and pane IDs must be unique per lifetime. AppModel activeTab commands remain global until the next bridge.

## 2026-11-19 — Real Compare drag image and narrow native divider cursors

**Compare:** `CompareColumnDragController` snapshots the actual visible union of a column's registered native regions; source dims only after capture. Native NSDraggingSession frame uses window coordinates plus the first mouse-drag translation, preserving pickup rather than centering. Uses backing-scale bitmaps, explicit opaque window-background composition, closed-hand only during its own session, same-controller `.move` destinations and active-tab/Compare/payload/history-key drop guards. Original NSGestureRecognizerRepresentable action was delivered on mouse-up, so the custom recognizer begins inside mouseDragged; ordinary single clicks remain undelayed and double clicks fail the lift recognizer. No cursor warping, global input monitor or private APIs. Trace/image output requires DEBUG + --qa-library + --qa-drag-trace and stays in the named QA library.

The reader yields only near these boundaries. Passive tracking areas attach directly to each native split, accept no hits, replace no delegates and remain unchanged when geometry is unchanged. Cursor exits yield to the entered native control/text. Inspector width probe also uses arrangedSubviews. Standalone fixtures cover decorations, hidden panes, invalid overlap, both glass edges and horizontal split; command below. ## 2026-11-19 — Consecutive, pinned and stacked Compare drag follow-through

Source `bbca520f`; migration remains ACTIVE. Previous published718d9e2e exact CI44665586360 completed SUCCESS for both jobs, including the real AppKit divider regression. That is baseline evidence, not the new head’s result.

**Actual failure and fix:** aligned reordering worked once, then a later native request captured the right column while the initial pickup was in the left. Multiple NSGestureRecognizerRepresentable instances can share one hosting view, so recognizer closure identity cannot identify the live column after reorder. Controller now groups actual live visible registered fragments by payload and requires exactly one source whose actual fragment contains the original pickup (event location minus initial translation). It captures that source and reports its resolved payload back for the lift state. Using only a union rectangle would be ambiguous for vertically interleaved stacked editions, so source picking tests individual fragments. Native completion explicitly ends the recognizer and clears its state because NSDraggingSession can consume mouse-up; a late mouse-up cannot fail an already completed lift.

**Stacked source appearance:** clipping the render to source fragments prevents other editions’ intervening verses from appearing in the drag image. Stacked edition headers and whole passage blocks register regions; parent block dims once and inner text does not dim again. Actual generated final LXX bitmap shows its header/edition labels/two body passages, blanking intervening KJVA passages. Blue verse-number controls are not clearly present in that cache image: keep their pixel fidelity, selected/dashed/highlighted controls and full2x/light pipeline OPEN; do not call the whole snapshot pixel-exact. Current display backing scale remains1. Physical OS-owned moving-image/cursor feel remains unverified.

**Runtime checks:** four consecutive aligned left→right moves alternated KJVA/LXX each time with no sheet. Three pinned LXX/independent moves alternated correctly. Invalid drop into sidebar plus Escape ended operation0 and retained order; the following two moves ended operation16 and alternated/restored order. Strong’sON then a real rendered G2532 click opened the Lexicon inspector despite AX marking links disabled. Strong’sOFF/panelOFF restored. Added one temporary duplicate KJVA Exodus32 comparison column through Add Another Passage→chapter32, unpinned that column and opened inspector to force a three-column stacked aligned layout. LXX moved from second→first and first→second on the final source; generated functional PNG inspected directly. Closed only the extra third comparison column through its own Close Column action. No top-level tab was added or removed.

**CUA pitfall:** native drag can complete either before or during the final synthetic click. Clicking a header after a completed drag opens its passage picker; cancel it rather than treating that sheet as a failed drag. If a drag is still active, the completion click must be on the intended target. For vertically stacked return, clicking the first body would target the source and correctly produce operation0. Use the actual target’s body coordinates and observe order plus native ended operation. Final successful stacked sequence used second header[1000,260]→first target body[1000,500], then first header[1000,168]→second target body[1000,686], with the completion click at each target. These coordinates apply only to this observed1488×665/three-column/inspector layout. Fresh screenshot/AX required after layout changes. A window-resize drag did not resize the synthetic window, so no narrow-window resize claim.

### 2026-11-20 — Claude Code: QA incident (Mac) and the rule it adds

While testing the image resize menu, my QA app's window had not opened yet, and one `cliclick` right-click at screen (560,380) landed on the **installed Electron "Berean" window (PID 78736, Michael's real library)**. It opened that app's verse context menu on Romans 1:24. I dismissed it with Escape sent only to that PID; **nothing was selected or changed** (screenshot confirmed: menu closed, only a hover tint). Rule for every AI: before any coordinate click on the Mac, verify the QA app's PID is frontmost **and** owns the window at that point (helper written: scratchpad `safeclick.sh`). The on-screen window list can disagree with what is drawn, so when in doubt, do not click on the Mac. Prefer the isolated simulator. Further Mac GUI checks of Image Size were skipped for this reason.
